So, no, I haven't really had trouble focusing, I just don't care about what I'm doing while I'm there.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that things you usually enjoy, like going out on the water or working on your sculpture, don’t give you any pleasure anymore?\n\nPatient: Yeah, that's exactly it. It's just... everything feels pointless right now.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of time when you felt unusually high, extremely energized, or overly irritable where you didn’t need much sleep or felt like you could take on the world?\n\nPatient: No, definitely not. It just hit me all at once after she was gone.\n\nDoctor: Before this recent time, have you ever had a period of at least two weeks where you felt so down or lost interest in things that it affected your daily life, like work or hobbies?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. It's really only been about a month since she left for basic training, and that's when all this started.
